- 批注类型: WordComment
- 批注人: 作者
- 人工意见: 内容后面也写了，看是否要整合
- 选中文本: Power-on self-testWhen you install and start the software for the first time or restart the computer and the sequencer, power-on self-test is triggered to check the USB peripheral, network cable connection, firmware version, mounted disk folder specification, and algorithm service one by one. The login interface is displayed when the test is successful, and a message that indicates the exceptions is displayed if the test fails. Corresponding solutions can be found in “Chapter 7 Troubleshooting”.2.6.2.2 LoginAfter passing the power-on self-test, the system will automatically redirect to the login interface, where users can enter the username and password to access the main interface of the software. The CycloneMaster control software supports two types of account logins: user mode and administrator mode. User mode has sequencing operation permissions. Administrator mode has sequencing operation, advanced configuration, viewing log details, and system maintenance permissions. It can enable module check and switch the software window display mode.
- 附近正文: Power-on self-testWhen you install and start the software for the first time or restart the computer and the sequencer, power-on self-test is triggered to check the USB peripheral, network cable connection, firmware version, mounted disk folder specification, and algorithm service one by one. The login interface is displayed when the test is successful, and a message that indicates the exceptions is displayed if the test fails. Corresponding solutions can be found in “Chapter 7 Troubleshooting”.2.6.2.2 LoginAfter passing the power-on self-test, the system will automatically redirect to the login interface, where users can enter the username and password to access the main interface of the software. The CycloneMaster control software supports two types of account logins: user mode and administrator mode. User mode has sequencing operation permissions. - 附近正文: Q 同一物料同一批次是否支持录入多个不同有效期？ A 不支持。入库时系统强制校验，相同物料编码与相同批次号只能对应一个有效期，以确保批 次效期管理的准确性，避免混淆。 Q 分批出库时，如何记录每批操作？ A 每次出库操作均生成一条出库登记单，并自动关联至原出库申请单。申请单详情页中按照物 料编码维度统计出库记录，点击可查看分批出库的出库登记单明细，便于申请人员追溯物料 出库情况。
